VIE_Change_Main_Favoritism = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = Main_Favoritism
		value = favoritism_change
		tooltip = VIE_Favoritism_Adj_tt
	}
	refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
}

VIE_Change_Alt_Favoritism = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = Alt_Favoritism
		value = favoritism_change
		tooltip = VIE_Alt_Favoritism_Adj_tt
	}
	refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
}

VIE_Change_Domestic_Influence = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIE_Influence
		value = influence_change
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		var = FRA_Influence
		value = influence_change
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_Influence_Adj_tt
	refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
}

# AI-only management of the French coup, driven by the VIE_ai_french_coup game rule.
# Runs daily from on_daily_VIE. Never touches a human-controlled VIE.
# influence_change adds to VIE_Influence and subtracts from FRA_Influence (see above),
# so a NEGATIVE influence_change raises FRA_Influence.
VIE_ai_french_coup_gamerule = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			has_game_rule = { rule = VIE_ai_french_coup option = VIE_FRENCH_COUP_AI_PROTECTED }
			check_variable = { FRA_Influence < 20 }
		}
		# Soft floor: lift FRA_Influence back to 25, keeping the pair summed to 100.
		# influence_change = FRA_Influence - 25 (negative), so FRA_Influence -> 25.
		set_temp_variable = { influence_change = FRA_Influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = influence_change value = 25 }
		VIE_Change_Domestic_Influence = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			has_game_rule = { rule = VIE_ai_french_coup option = VIE_FRENCH_COUP_AI_GUARANTEED }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_FRA_Coup }
			check_variable = { FRA_Influence > 5 }
		}
		# Driver: push FRA_Influence down until it crosses the coup threshold (< 11),
		# at which point ProFrench_Coup activates through its normal path.
		set_temp_variable = { influence_change = 3 }
		VIE_Change_Domestic_Influence = yes
	}
}

vietnam_cochinchina_unification_war = {
	CCC = {
		var:Sphere_Leader = {
			remove_from_array = {
				sphere_tag_list = PREV.id
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = Is_In_Sphere
		set_variable = {
			Sphere_Leader = 0
		}
		leave_faction = yes
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		VIE = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = CCC
				autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
		TAI = {
			diplomatic_relation	= { country = CCC relation = military_access active = no }
		}
		NUN = {
			diplomatic_relation	= { country = CCC relation = military_access active = no }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ally_with = FRA
			}
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = antagonize
				id = PREV
				value = 100
			}
			CCC = {
				diplomatic_relation	= { country = PREV relation = military_access active = no }
			}
		}
	}
	VIE = {
		declare_war_on = {
			target = CCC
			type = annex_everything
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = FUL
					original_tag = FRA
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = PREV.PREV
				enemy = CCC
				hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			}
		}
	}
}

reset_favoritism_minigame = {
	VIE = {
		set_variable = { Alt_Favoritism = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Cuong_De
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Cuong_De
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Xuan
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Xuan
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phan_Long
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phan_Long
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Van_Huu
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Van_Huu
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Tam
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Van_Tam
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phuc_Buu_Loc
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Phuc_Buu_Loc
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Quang_Vinh
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Tran_Quang_Vinh
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Bao_Toan
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Nguyen_Bao_Toan
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Dinh_Xuan_Quang
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Dinh_Xuan_Quang
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Diem
			}
			clr_country_flag = VIE_Opposition_Diem
		}
		refresh_favoritism_minigame = yes
	}
}

refresh_favoritism_minigame = {
	VIE = {
		add_to_variable = { VIE_Refresh_Variable = 1 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = VIE_Influence
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = FRA_Influence
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = Main_Favoritism
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = Alt_Favoritism
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
	}
	MLA = {
		add_to_variable = { MLA_Refresh_Variable = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = PRC_Influence }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = PRC_Influence
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = CHI_Influence }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = CHI_Influence
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = Main_MLA_Support }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = Main_MLA_Support
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = Alt_MLA_Support }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = Alt_MLA_Support
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
	set_country_flag = activate_sect_menu
	country_event = VIE_Mechanics.2
}

	set_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation_number = Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { Cao_Dai_Loyalty_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Cao_Dai_Loyalty_number = Cao_Dai_Loyalty }
	set_variable = { Cao_Dai_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Cao_Dai_Annihilation_number = Cao_Dai_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { Harmonism_Loyalty_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Harmonism_Loyalty_number = Harmonism_Loyalty }
	set_variable = { Harmonism_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Harmonism_Annihilation_number = Harmonism_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Loyalty_number = 100 }

	set_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Annihilation_number = 100 }
	subtract_from_variable = { Hoa_Hao_Annihilation_number = Hoa_Hao_Annihilation }

	set_variable = { UMDC_Annihilation_number = 100 }

}

VIE_annex_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				has_country_flag = Easy_CCC_Unification
			}
			FROM = {
				original_tag = CCC
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = Easy_CCC_Unification
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = FROM
			}
			set_compliance = 35
			set_resistance = 25
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		ROOT = {
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = FROM
				}
				set_compliance = 10
				set_resistance = 50
			}
		}
	}
}

Phuong_Mutiny = {
	CCC = {
		set_country_flag = CCC_Phuong_Mutiny
		set_cosmetic_tag = CCC_Phuong
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_nam_viet_guard }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_viet_binh_doan }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CCC_cochinchina_sect_militaries }
		transfer_state = 1285
		VIE = {
			set_province_controller = 7331
			set_province_controller = 13699
			set_province_controller = 4284
			set_province_controller = 16438
			set_province_controller = 4223
			set_province_controller = 13703
			set_province_controller = 7303
			set_province_controller = 13700
			set_province_controller = 13701
			set_province_controller = 13706
			set_province_controller = 12288
			set_province_controller = 1423
			set_province_controller = 1396
			set_province_controller = 13708
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = neutrality
			popularity = 100
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
			long_name = "Cao-Dai Bao-Tran Quan-Quyen"
			name = "CD-BT-QQ"
		}
		CCC_Nguyen_Thanh_Phuong = {
			set_nationality = CCC
			add_field_marshal_role = {
				traits = { irregular_officer commando camouflage_expert ww2_veteran CaoDai_Member CuongDe_Comrade }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 1
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 1
				logistics_skill = 4
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = military_junta
				}
			}
		}
		division_template = {
			name = "Forces Armées Caodaistes"
			division_names_group = VIE_MIL_01
			template_counter = 203
			priority = 1
			is_locked = no

			regiments = {
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				militia = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				militia = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				militia = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
			support = {
				engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				light_artillery_support = { x = 1 y = 0 }
			}
		}
		VIE = {
			every_owned_state = {
				add_claim_by = CCC
			}
		}
		FUL = {
			every_owned_state = {
				add_claim_by = CCC
			}
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = VIE
			type = annex_everything
		}
		1285 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"1st Co-Thanh Ve\" division_template = \"Forces Armées Caodaistes\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 0.5"
				allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				prioritize_location = 16634
				owner = CCC
			}
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"2nd Co-Thanh Ve\" division_template = \"Forces Armées Caodaistes\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 0.5"
				allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = yes
				prioritize_location = 16635
				owner = CCC
			}
		}
	}
}

End_Phuong_Mutiny = {
	CCC = {
		clr_country_flag = CCC_Phuong_Mutiny
		set_cosmetic_tag = CCC_FRA
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		kill_country_leader = yes
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_claimed_by = CCC
				NOT = {
					state = 286
					state = 1285
					state = 1752
					state = 1753
					state = 1754
					state = 1755
					state = 1804
				}
			}
			remove_claim_by = CCC
		}
	}
	VIE = {
		annex_country = {
			target = CCC
			transfer_troops = no
		}
	}
}

NLF_Victory = {
	NLF = {
		annex_country = {
			target = VIE
			transfer_troops = no
		}
		annex_country = {
			target = FUL
			transfer_troops = no
		}
		annex_country = {
			target = CCC
			transfer_troops = no
		}
		set_capital	= { state = 286	}
		set_cosmetic_tag = NLF_VIE
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = NLF_Nguyen_Binh
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = communism
				elections_allowed = no
				long_name = "Chinh Phu Cach Mang Lam Thoi"
				name = "CPCMLT"
			}
			NLF_Nguyen_Binh = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = ho_chi_minh_thought
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					long_name = "Chinh Phu Cach Mang Lam Thoi"
					name = "CPCMLT"
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = trotskyism
					elections_allowed = no
					long_name = "Chinh Phu Cach Mang Lam Thoi - Cong Nhan"
					name = "CPCMLT-CN"
				}
				set_popularities = {
					democratic = 0
					communism = 50
					fascism = 0
					nationalist = 0
					monarchism = 0
					neutrality = 0
					islamism = 0
					radical_shiaism = 0
					Social_Democratic = 0
					Social_Liberalism = 0
					Salafism = 0
					Liberal_Conservatism = 0
					conservative = 0
					Christian_Democratic = 0
					socialist = 0
					centrist = 0
					liberal = 0
					trotskyism = 50
					maoism = 0
				}
				NLF_Kieu_Cong_Cung = {
					add_country_leader_role = {
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = {
							ideology = syndicalism
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Vietnam Factionalism Tooltips
binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = {
	set_temp_variable = { inverse_number = binh_xuyen_loyalty_change }
	multiply_temp_variable = {
		inverse_number = -1
		tooltip = change_binh_xuyen_loyalty_TT
	}
	add_to_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty = binh_xuyen_loyalty_change }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = Binh_Xuyen_Loyalty
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}
binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = {
	set_temp_variable = { inverse_number = binh_xuyen_annihilation_change }
	multiply_temp_variable = {

		tooltip = change_binh_xuyen_annihilation_TT
	}
	add_to_variable = { Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation = binh_xuyen_annihilation_change }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = Binh_Xuyen_Annihilation
		min = 0
		max = 100
	}
}

vietnam_faction_ui_decision_click = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 100 }
		}
		add_political_power = -45
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = -15 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = VIE_Recruit_BinhXuyen_Militias_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			capital_scope = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Cong-an Xung-phong Brigade\" division_template = \"Forces Supplétives de Binh-Xuyen\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1"
					owner = VIE
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Cong-an Xung-phong Brigade\" division_template = \"Forces Supplétives de Binh-Xuyen\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1"
					owner = VIE
				}
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Cong-an Xung-phong Brigade\" division_template = \"Forces Supplétives de Binh-Xuyen\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1"
					owner = VIE
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = Decision_100_Completed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 101 }
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_Warlord_Status_2 = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = -8 }

				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}

			add_building_construction = {

				level = 1

			}
		}
		set_country_flag = Decision_102_Completed
	}
	else_if = {

			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 103 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = -15 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		set_country_flag = Decision_103_Completed
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_political_power = -35

				level = 1

		}

		}

		binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
		add_political_power = -30
		add_war_support = 0.05
		add_stability = 0.03
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 107 }	#Recognize Binh-Xuyen Monopoly
		}
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = -50 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		add_political_power = -200
		add_stability = -0.15
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 108 }	#Contain Binh-Xuyen Influence
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = 5 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = -3 }
		binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 109 }	#Attack Binh-Xuyen's Army
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_command_power = -5
		custom_effect_tooltip = Attack_Binh_Xuyen_Forces_TT
		set_variable = {
			VIE_success_chance = random
		}
		set_variable = {
			VIE_Conflict_ID = 1
		}
		add_to_variable = { VIE_success_chance = VIE_Sect_Pity_1 } # streak protection, grows on each failure
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = 5 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		set_variable = {
			show_vie_sect_conflict = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_success_chance > 0.25
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				VIE_Sect_Conflict_Prompt = 1
			}
			hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = -10 }
					binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
				set_variable = { VIE_Sect_Pity_1 = 0 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				VIE_Sect_Conflict_Prompt = 2
			}
			add_political_power = 15 # partial refund on a failed operation
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = 2 }
				binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
				add_to_variable = { VIE_Sect_Pity_1 = 0.15 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 110 }	#Seize Binh-Xuyen Facilities
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_command_power = -5
		custom_effect_tooltip = Attempt_to_Seize_Binh_Xuyen_Facilities_TT
		set_variable = {
			VIE_success_chance = random
		}
		set_variable = {
			VIE_Conflict_ID = 1
		}
		add_to_variable = { VIE_success_chance = VIE_Sect_Pity_1 } # streak protection, grows on each failure
		set_variable = {
			show_vie_sect_conflict = 1
		}
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = 15 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					VIE_success_chance > 0.39
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				VIE_Sect_Conflict_Prompt = 3
			}
			set_country_flag = Seized_Binh_Xuyen_Facilities
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = -25 }
				binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
				set_variable = { VIE_Sect_Pity_1 = 0 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				VIE_Sect_Conflict_Prompt = 4
			}
			add_political_power = 10 # partial refund on a failed operation
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_annihilation_change = 5 }
				binh_xuyen_annihilation_change_tt = yes
				add_to_variable = { VIE_Sect_Pity_1 = 0.15 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { vietnam_factionalism_decisions_list^i = 111 }	#Broadcast Pro-Government Messaging
		}
		add_political_power = -15
		set_temp_variable = { binh_xuyen_loyalty_change = 5 }
		binh_xuyen_loyalty_change_tt = yes
	}

# VIE State Integration Decisions

SendTroops_Option_enabled = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VIE_Can_Send_Army_trigger = yes
		}
		set_variable = { VIE_Can_Send_Army = 2 }
		else = {
			set_variable = { VIE_Can_Send_Army = 1 }
		}
	}
}

VIE_OpenDiplomacy_effect = {
	if = {

			VIE_Can_Open_Diplomacy_trigger = yes

		}

VIE_get_state_info = {
	1752 = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				is_core_of = VIE
				is_fully_controlled_by = VIE
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 2 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = CCC
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 1 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 2 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				is_controlled_by = NLF
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 7 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance > 75
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 6 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance < 76
				resistance > 50
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 5 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE
				NOT = { is_core_of = VIE }
				has_resistance = yes
				resistance < 51
				resistance > 25
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = NLF }
			}
			VIE = {
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Status = 4 }
				set_variable = { VIE_State_1_Owner = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = VIE

xiangqi_add_legal_attack = {

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count > 4 } }
		hidden_effect = { } # no-op
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 1 }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 1 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_1_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_1_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_2_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_2_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_3_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_4_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_5_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
	}
}
xiangqi_add_legal_move = {
	# Expects:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col

	# If already at cap, do nothing (prevents the "last direction disappears" illusion)
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
			xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes
		}
		xiangqi_add_legal_attack = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
			xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes
		}
		xiangqi_add_legal_attack = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count > 24 } }
		hidden_effect = { } # no-op
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 1 }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 1 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_1_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_1_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_2_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_2_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_3_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_3_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_4_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_4_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_5_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_5_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_6_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_6_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_7_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_7_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_8_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_8_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_9_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_9_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_10_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_10_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 11 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_11_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_11_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 12 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_12_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_12_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 13 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_13_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_13_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 14 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_14_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_14_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 15 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_15_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_15_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 16 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_16_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_16_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }

		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 20 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_20_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_20_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 21 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_21_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_21_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }

		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count = 23 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_23_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_23_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col } }

	}
}


xiangqi_government_elephant_generate_legal_moves = {
	# Only generate if alive
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Cache current position
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col }

		# Government is the TOP side: Elephant must stay on rows 1-5
		# River boundary is between 5 and 6.

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 1: (row + 2, col + 2)
		# Eye: (row + 1, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }

		# Eye square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 2: (row + 2, col - 2)
		# Eye: (row + 1, col - 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 3: (row - 2, col + 2)
		# Eye: (row - 1, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 4: (row - 2, col - 2)
		# Eye: (row - 1, col - 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 6 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}
xiangqi_government_horse_generate_legal_moves = {
	# Only generate if alive
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Cache current position
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col }

		# ======================================================
		# Candidate 1: (row + 2, col + 1)  Leg: (row + 1, col)
		# ======================================================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }   # rows 1-9
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }   # cols 1-10

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row + 2, col - 1)  Leg: (row + 1, col)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 2, col + 1)  Leg: (row - 1, col)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 2, col - 1)  Leg: (row - 1, col)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ======================================================
		# Candidate 5: (row + 1, col + 2)  Leg: (row, col + 1)
		# ======================================================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = 1 }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 1, col + 2)  Leg: (row, col + 1)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = 1 }
		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row + 1, col - 2)  Leg: (row, col - 1)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = -1 }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 1, col - 2)  Leg: (row, col - 1)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = -1 }
		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}
	}
}
xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }
		# =====================
		# LEFT (row - 1, -2, ...)
		# =====================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}
xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }

    # RIGHT (row + 1, +2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

# ============================================================
# GOVERNMENT CANON ATTACKS (capture targets only)
# Rule:
#   - Scan outward in one direction.
#   - First occupied square sets "screen_found = 1" and continues.
#   - After screen_found, the FIRST occupied square is capturable IF enemy.
#   - Stop after that first post-screen occupied square (hit or not).
# ============================================================

xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_up = {
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 11 } } # safety cap

        # Target: col - iter
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Off-board?
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Check square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # If no screen yet: first occupied becomes screen, do NOT add capture
        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}
xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_down = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }

xiangqi_government_canon_generate_attacks = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } }

        # Do NOT clear legal moves here if you're combining moves+attacks in one list.
        # If you're keeping separate "legal_move" lists, call the right clear here.

        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_up = yes
        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_down = yes
        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_left = yes
        xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_right = yes
    }
}

xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves = {
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_down = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_up = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = yes


	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_up = yes
	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_down = yes
	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_left = yes
	xiangqi_government_canon_attacks_right = yes
}


xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }
		# =====================
		# LEFT (row - 1, -2, ...)
		# =====================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}
xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }

    # RIGHT (row + 1, +2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}
xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_up = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }

    # =====================
    # UP (column -1, -2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves = {
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_down = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_right = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_up = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_left = 0 }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_down = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_down = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_right = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_right = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_up = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_up = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves_left = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_left = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }
}



xiangqi_government_soldier_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col }

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 1: Forward (row, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Side moves only after river (origin_col > 5)
		# ---------------------------------------
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col > 5 } }

			# Candidate 2: Right (row + 1, col)
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}

			# Candidate 3: Left (row - 1, col)
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

xiangqi_government_general_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col }

		# Government (Top) palace:
		# Rows (horizontal): 4..6  => row > 3 AND row < 7
		# Cols (vertical):   1..3  => col > 0 AND col < 4

		# ===============================
		# Move LEFT (row - 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ===============================
		# Move RIGHT (row + 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ===============================
		# Move UP (column - 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ===============================
		# Move DOWN (column + 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 4 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_friendly_government = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}
	}
}

Potential_Move_1_click_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}
