	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }

		# Cleanup
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Optional: end selection
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	}
}

xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_move = {
	# Expects:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_move_index (1..24)
	# Uses:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side (1 gov / 2 reformist)
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type (1 general, 2 soldier_front, 3 elephant, 4 horse, 5 chariot, 6 canon, 7 soldier)

	# --- Resolve target row/col from move index ---
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 0 }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 1 } }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 11 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 12 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 13 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 14 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 15 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 16 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 17 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 18 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 19 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 20 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 21 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 22 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 23 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 24 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_col } }
	else = {
		# Invalid index, just bail safely
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 1 }

	}

	# Optional safety: ignore clicking an "empty" move dot
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 1 }
	}

	# --- Apply to selected piece (your same mapping) ---

	# Government (side = 1)
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 0 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = Playing_Nhan_Van_VIN
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_moved_side = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_moved_type = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type }
		}
	}

	#Reformist
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_abort_move = 0 }
			check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		}
		set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_moved_side = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_moved_type = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = Playing_Government_VIN
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_moved_side = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_moved_type = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type }
		}
	}


	# Cleanup selection + move dots

	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 0 }
	set_variable = {xiangqi_selected_row = 0 }
	set_variable = {xiangqi_selected_col = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }
}


#Reformist Moves
xiangqi_reformist_elephant_generate_legal_moves = {
	# Only generate if alive
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Cache current position
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col }

		# River boundary is between 5 and 6.

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 1: (row + 2, col + 2)
		# Eye: (row + 1, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }

		# Eye square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 2: (row + 2, col - 2)
		# Eye: (row + 1, col - 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 3: (row - 2, col + 2)
		# Eye: (row - 1, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_col = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_eye_col }
		if = {

				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 5 }	#River Rule
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }    # cols 1-9


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied
				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}
		}
		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 4: (row - 2, col - 2)
		# Eye: (row - 1, col - 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_eye_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }

xiangqi_reformist_horse_generate_legal_moves = {
	# Only generate if alive
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		# Cache current position
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col }

		# ======================================================
		# Candidate 1: (row + 2, col + 1)  Leg: (row + 1, col)
		# ======================================================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }   # rows 1-9
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }   # cols 1-10

				# Destination must not be friendly-occupied

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row + 2, col - 1)  Leg: (row + 1, col)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 2, col + 1)  Leg: (row - 1, col)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 2, col - 1)  Leg: (row - 1, col)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -2 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ======================================================
		# Candidate 5: (row + 1, col + 2)  Leg: (row, col + 1)
		# ======================================================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = 1 }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row - 1, col + 2)  Leg: (row, col + 1)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = 1 }
		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# (row + 1, col - 2)  Leg: (row, col - 1)
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_col = -1 }

		# Leg square must be empty
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_leg_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {

				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -2 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_leg_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}
	}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }
		# =====================
		# LEFT (row - 1, -2, ...)
		# =====================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}
xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    # RIGHT (row + 1, +2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}
xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_up = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    # =====================
    # UP (column -1, -2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}
xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_down = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    # =====================
    # DOWN (column +1, +2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}
# ============================================================
# GOVERNMENT CANON ATTACKS (capture targets only)
# Rule:
#   - Scan outward in one direction.
#   - First occupied square sets "screen_found = 1" and continues.
#   - After screen_found, the FIRST occupied square is capturable IF enemy.
#   - Stop after that first post-screen occupied square (hit or not).
# ============================================================

xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_up = {
    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 11 } } # safety cap

        # Target: col - iter
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        # Off-board?
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Check square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # If no screen yet: first occupied becomes screen, do NOT add capture
        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}
xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_down = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 11 } }

        # Target: col + iter
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 10 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_left = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col }

    set_temp_variable = { break = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } }

        # Target: row - iter
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 0 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_canon_screen_found = 1 }
                xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
                }
                hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
            }

            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_attacks = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } }

        # Do NOT clear legal moves here if you're combining moves+attacks in one list.
        # If you're keeping separate "legal_move" lists, call the right clear here.

        xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_up = yes
        xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_down = yes
        xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_left = yes
        xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_right = yes
    }
}

xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves = {
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_down = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_right = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_up = yes

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
    xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves_left = yes


	xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_up = yes
	xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_down = yes
	xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_left = yes
	xiangqi_reformist_canon_attacks_right = yes
}


xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves_left = {
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

		# Cache origin
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col }
		# =====================
		# LEFT (row - 1, -2, ...)
		# =====================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

		while_loop_effect = {
			break = break
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } check_variable = { break_loop = 0 } }

			# Target square
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Bounds
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
						check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes } }
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
				set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
			}

			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
		}
}
xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves_right = {
	add_to_variable = { xiangqi_debug_dir_calls = 1 }

    # Cache origin
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col }

    # RIGHT (row + 1, +2, ...)
    # =====================
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }

    while_loop_effect = {
		break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 10 } # safety cap
			check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        # Compute target square
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

        # Stop if off board
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 1 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 9 }
                }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Set square to check
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

        # Legal move if not friendly-occupied
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
            }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
        }

        # Stop ray if ANY piece blocks
        if = {
            limit = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes }
			set_variable = { break_loop = 1 }
            set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
        }

        # Next square
        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }
}

xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves = {
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_down = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_right = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_up = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_left = 0 }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves_down = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_down = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves_right = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_right = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves_up = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_up = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }

	set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
	xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves_left = yes
	set_variable = { xiangqi_debug_after_left = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_count }
}



xiangqi_reformist_soldier_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col }

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 1: Forward (row, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Side moves only after river (origin_col > 6)
		# ---------------------------------------
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col < 6 } }

			# Candidate 2: Right (row + 1, col)
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}

			# Candidate 3: Left (row - 1, col)
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
xiangqi_reformist_Soldier_Front_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col }

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Candidate 1: Forward (row, col + 1)
		# ---------------------------------------
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = -1 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }
			}
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
			}

		}

		# ---------------------------------------
		# Side moves only after river (origin_col > 5)
		# ---------------------------------------
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col < 6 } }

			# Candidate 2: Right (row + 1, col)
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}

			# Candidate 3: Left (row - 1, col)
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
			add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = -1 }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 10 }
				}
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
					}
					hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
xiangqi_reformist_general_generate_legal_moves = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_alive = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col }

		# Reformist (Bottom) palace:
		# Rows (horizontal): 4..6  => row > 3 AND row < 7
		# Cols (vertical):   1..3  => col > 0 AND col < 4

		# ===============================
		# Move LEFT (row - 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }

				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ===============================
		# Move RIGHT (row + 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 1 }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ===============================
		# Move UP (column - 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }

				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}

		# ===============================
		# Move DOWN (column + 1)
		# ===============================
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }
		add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 1 }

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 3 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row < 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 7 }
				check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < 11 }


				NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = { xiangqi_add_legal_move = yes }
		}
	}
}


xiangqi_execute_selected_piece_attack = {
	# Expects:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_check_row
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_check_col
	# Uses:
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side (1 gov, 2 reformist)
	#   ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type (1 general, 2 vanguard, 3 elephant, 4 horse, 5 chariot, 6 canon, 7 soldier)

	# -------------------------
	# Resolve damage from piece variables
	# -------------------------
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = 0 }

	# Government attacker
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_attack_point } }
	}
	# Reformist attacker
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_attack_point } }
	}

	# -------------------------
	# Apply damage (only if damage is valid)
	# -------------------------
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage > 0 } }
		set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_attacker_side = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side }
		hidden_effect = { xiangqi_apply_damage_at_square = yes }
	}

	# -------------------------
	# Cleanup (always)
	# -------------------------
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_attacks = yes }
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = 0 }
}

xiangqi_ai_reformist_execute_chosen_attack = {
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_chosen_type }

	# regenerate attacks for that piece so slots are guaranteed valid
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_generate_for_selected_type = yes }

	# load chosen slot into tmp_check row/col
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col } }

	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_execute_selected_piece_attack = yes }

	# AI cleanup flags
	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 1 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 0 }
}

	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }

	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 0 }
}

xiangqi_resolve_piece_at_square = {
    # Expects:
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_check_row
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_check_col
    # Sets:
    #   xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 0/1/2
    #   xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 0..7  (your type ids)
    #   xiangqi_tmp_target_is_front = 0/1

    set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 0 }
    set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 0 }
    set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_is_front = 0 }

    # -------------------------
    # Government
    # -------------------------
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 1 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 2 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_is_front = 1 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 4 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 5 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 6 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row > 0 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 7 }
    }

    # -------------------------
    # Reformist (only if none found)
    # -------------------------
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 0 } }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_is_front = 1 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 3 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 4 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 5 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 6 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row > 0 }
            }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 2 }
            set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 7 }
        }
    }
}
xiangqi_apply_damage_at_square = {
    # Expects:
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_check_row
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_check_col
    # Uses:
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_side
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type

    # Optional debug
    set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = 0 }
    set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_hit_success = 0 }

	# -------------------------
	# Determine damage from attacker
	# -------------------------
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_attack_point } }
	}
	else_if = {
		# Reformist attacker
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_attack_point } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_temp_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_damage = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_attack_point } }
	}
	else = { }
	# -------------------------
	# Resolve target piece on square
	# -------------------------
	hidden_effect = { xiangqi_resolve_piece_at_square = yes }
	# =========================
	# Government target
	# =========================
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_side = 1 } }

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 1 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col = -1 }
				complete_national_focus = VIN_Let_Hundred_Flowers_Bloom
				remove_nhan_van_minigame_to_ui = yes
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 2 } }
			# Vanguard only (Soldier_Front)
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 2 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 3 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 3 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 4 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 4 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 5 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_alive = -1 }
