				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 5 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 6 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 6 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 7 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Government_ID = 7 }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 2 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 2 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 3 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 3 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 4 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 4 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 5 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 5 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 6 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 6 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 7 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_health_point = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = -1 }
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 7 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_piece_type = 1 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_health_point = xiangqi_tmp_damage }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_health_point < 1 } }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_alive = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = -1 }
				set_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = -1 }
				complete_national_focus = VIN_Destroy_Reactionary_Thoughts
				remove_nhan_van_minigame_to_ui = yes
				add_to_array = { Fallen_Reformist_ID = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}
xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_attack = {
    # Expects:
    #   ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_attack_index (1..5)
    # Uses:
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_side / xiangqi_selected_piece_type for damage source
    # Applies:
    #   xiangqi_execute_selected_piece_attack (expects tmp_check_row/col)

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_attack_index = 1 } }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_col }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_attack_index = 2 } }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_col }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_attack_index = 3 } }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_attack_index = 4 } }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col }
    }
    else = {
        # Default to slot 5 (or clamp)
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col }
    }

    # Execute the damage pipeline
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_execute_selected_piece_attack = yes }

    # Cleanup
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_attack_moves = yes }
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_id = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_attack_index = 0 }
}

#AI Movement and Processing
xiangqi_ai_reformist_generate_for_selected_type = {
    # Expects:
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1..7

    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_attack_moves = yes } # if exists
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_Soldier_Front_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_elephant_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_horse_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_soldier_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_general_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
}
xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }

    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_generate_for_selected_type = yes }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count > 0 } }

        # Determine our attack power for this selected type (Reformist)
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = 0 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_attack_point } }

        # Pick best slot 1..5
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_score = -999 }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        while_loop_effect = {
            break = break
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter < 6 } } # 1..5

            # Stop if past count
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter > xiangqi_legal_attack_count } }
                set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
            }

            # Load this attack slot into tmp_check_row/col
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 2 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 3 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 4 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 5 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col }
            }

            # Evaluate target at this square (sets target_value, target_hp, target_exists)
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_eval_government_target_at_tmp_square = yes }

            # Score
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -999 }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 } }

                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 0 }

                # Base: target value weight
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_target_value }

                # Prefer kills
                # kill if atk > hp OR atk = hp
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk > xiangqi_ai_target_hp }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = xiangqi_ai_target_hp }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 50 }
                }
                else = {
                    # Otherwise prefer dealing more relative damage
                    # (atk - hp) is negative; better if closer to 0
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_tmp_diff = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk }
                    subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_ai_tmp_diff = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_target_hp }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_tmp_diff } # less negative is better
                }

                # Small randomness to avoid always same choice
                random = { chance = 35 add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }
            }

            # Keep best
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score > xiangqi_ai_best_attack_score } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_score }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_iter }
            }

            add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index > 0 } }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index }
        }
    }
}
xiangqi_ai_eval_government_target_at_tmp_square = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 0 }

    # Must be occupied by enemy at all
    if = {
        limit = {
            xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            NOT = { xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes } # enemy for Reformist
        }

        # Canon
        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 10 }
        }
        # Chariot
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 9 }
        }
        # Horse
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 7 }
        }
        # Elephant
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 6 }
        }
        # Soldier Front
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 5 }
        }
        # Soldier
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 4 }
        }
        # General
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 8 }
        }
    }
}

xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = {
    # Expects:
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_type already set (1/2/3/4/5/6/7)
    # Assumes Reformist side
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }

    # Generate legal moves for this type
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_canon_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_chariot_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_horse_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_elephant_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_Soldier_Front_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_soldier_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_reformist_general_generate_legal_moves = yes } }

    # If none, bail
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count < 1 } }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
    }
    else = {
        # Score moves
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_move_index = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_score = -999 }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        while_loop_effect = {
            break = break
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter < 25 }
            }

            # stop if past count
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter > xiangqi_legal_move_count } }
                set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
            }

            # Resolve candidate row/col for this iter
            # (You already have this mapping elsewhere; keep it explicit.)
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }

            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 11 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 12 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 13 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 14 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 15 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 16 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 17 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 18 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 19 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 20 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 21 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 22 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 23 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 24 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_col } }

            # ---- Score it ----
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -2 } }

            # 1) Backtrack check (reject/huge penalty if equals last square)
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 0 }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Reformist_General_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Reformist_General_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 } }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -100 } # basically discard unless forced
            }

            # 2) Prefer forward progress for Reformist (smaller col = better)
            # Add +3 if col < current col, +1 if equal, -1 if col > current col
            # (We need current col per piece type)
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = 0 }

            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col } }

            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col < xiangqi_ai_curr_col } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 3 } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_ai_curr_col } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }
            else = { add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -1 } }

            # 3) Mild center preference (row closer to 5 is better)
            # +2 if row is 5, +1 if 4 or 6, else 0
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 5 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 2 } }
            else_if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 4 } check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 6 } } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }

            # 4) Random tie-breaker (0..1)
            random = {
                chance = 50
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 }
            }

            # Keep best
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score > xiangqi_ai_best_score } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_score }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_move_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_iter }
            }

            add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        }

        # If we found anything, execute it
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_move_index > 0 } }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_best_move_index }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_move = yes }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
        }

        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
    }
}

xiangqi_ai_reformist_take_turn = {
    # Reset AI state
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }

    # --------------------------
    # PHASE 1: TRY ATTACKS
    # --------------------------
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_attack_for_type = yes } }

    # --------------------------
    # PHASE 2: IF NO ATTACK, TRY MOVES
    # --------------------------
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } }

        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_try_move_for_type = yes } }
    }

    # --------------------------
    # PHASE 3: EXECUTE CHOSEN ACTION (THIS IS WHERE IT FIRES)
    # --------------------------
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 1 }
        }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_execute_chosen_attack = yes }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 0 }
        }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_execute_chosen_move = yes }
    }
}


xiangqi_ai_get_legal_move_at_index = {
    # Expects: xiangqi_tmp_iter (1..24)
    # Sets: xiangqi_tmp_target_row / xiangqi_tmp_target_col

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 } }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 11 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 12 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 13 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 14 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 15 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 16 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 17 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 18 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 19 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 20 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 21 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 22 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 23 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 24 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_col } }
}
xiangqi_ai_reformist_choose_nonreversal_move_index = {
    # Expects:
    #   xiangqi_ai_chosen_type (1..7)
    #   legal moves already generated into xiangqi_legal_move_*_row/col
    #
    # Sets:
    #   xiangqi_ai_chosen_index (1..24)

    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }

    # ----------------------------
    # Load last square for this type
    # ----------------------------
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = 0 }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_col } }

    # ----------------------------
    # Load current origin for this piece type (Reformist side)
    # ----------------------------
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = 0 }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row }       set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col } }

    # ----------------------------
    # Best-of scan
    # ----------------------------
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_score = -999 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_index = 0 }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 25 }
            check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_get_legal_move_at_index = yes } # sets tmp_target_row/col

        # Only consider real move dots
        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
            }

            # ----------------------------
            # Score this candidate
            # ----------------------------
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 0 }

			# (A) Huge penalty if it is immediate reversal, but only if we actually have history
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row > 0 }
					check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col > 0 }
				}

				# row match?
				set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
				subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_last_row }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = 0 } }

					# col match too?
					set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
					subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_last_col }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = 0 } }
						add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = -100 }
					}
				}
			}
            # (B) Prefer advancing toward Government (Reformist advances by DECREASING col)
			# compute delta_col = target_col - origin_col

			set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
			subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

			# Reformist advances by DECREASING col, so delta < 0 is good
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta < 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 }
							check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 }
						}
					}
					add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 7 }
				}
				else = { add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 4 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta > 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = -3 }
			}

            # (C) Centralize row (prefer row 5, then 4/6, then 3/7)
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 5 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 3 } }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 4 }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 6 }
                    }
                }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 2 }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 3 }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 7 }
                    }
                }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 1 }
            }

            # (D) Extra centralization for Chariot/Canon: prefer columns 5–6-ish (center lanes)
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } # Chariot
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } # Canon
                    }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 5 }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 6 }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 2 }
                }
                else_if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 4 }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 7 }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 1 }
                }
            }

            # (E) Deprioritize General wandering (keeps it from being the default mover)
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = -2 }
            }

            # ----------------------------
            # Keep best
            # ----------------------------
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score > xiangqi_tmp_best_score } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_score = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_score }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_index = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
            }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }

    # Finalize
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_index > 0 } }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_best_index }
    }
    else = {
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 1 } # ultra-safe fallback
    }
}

xiangqi_ai_reformist_execute_chosen_move = {
    # Expects:
    #   xiangqi_ai_chosen_type   (1..7)
    # Optional:
    #   xiangqi_ai_chosen_index  (if pre-chosen; otherwise we auto-pick)
    #
    # Uses:
    #   xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_X_row/col for anti-bounce

    # Force selection to Reformist
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 2 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_chosen_type }

    # Resolve current square (origin)
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = 0 }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row }       set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col } }

    # Store the square we are moving FROM (used to avoid immediate reversal next time)
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_prev_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_prev_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

    # Generate legal moves for this selected type
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_generate_for_selected_type = yes }

    # Only proceed if we actually have moves
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count > 0 } }

        # Choose index if not pre-set
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index < 1 } }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_reformist_choose_nonreversal_move_index = yes }
        }

        # Execute the move
        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_chosen_index }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_move = yes }

        # Record last square for this type (the square we came FROM)
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
    }
    else = {
        # No moves: cleanup
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }
    }

    # Cleanup AI pick vars (always)
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 0 }
}
#AI Movement and Processing (Government)

xiangqi_ai_government_generate_for_selected_type = {
    # Expects:
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1..7

    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_attack_moves = yes } # if exists
    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_Soldier_Front_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_elephant_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_horse_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_soldier_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_general_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
}

xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }

    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_generate_for_selected_type = yes }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_attack_count > 0 } }

        # Determine our attack power for this selected type (Government)
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = 0 }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_attack_point } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_attack_point } }

        # Pick best slot 1..5
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_score = -999 }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        while_loop_effect = {
            break = break
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter < 6 } } # 1..5

            # Stop if past count
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter > xiangqi_legal_attack_count } }
                set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
            }

            # Load this attack slot into tmp_check_row/col
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_1_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 2 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_2_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 3 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_3_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 4 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_4_col }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 5 } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_row }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_check_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_attack_5_col }
            }

            # Evaluate target at this square (sets target_value, target_hp, target_exists)
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_eval_reformist_target_at_tmp_square = yes }

            # Score
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -999 }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 } }

                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 0 }

                # Base: target value weight
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_target_value }

                # Prefer kills
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk > xiangqi_ai_target_hp }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk = xiangqi_ai_target_hp }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 50 }
                }
                else = {
                    # Otherwise prefer dealing more relative damage
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_tmp_diff = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_attacker_atk }
                    subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_ai_tmp_diff = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_target_hp }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_tmp_diff }
                }

                # Small randomness to avoid always same choice
                random = { chance = 35 add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }
            }

            # Keep best
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score > xiangqi_ai_best_attack_score } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_score }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_iter }
            }

            add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index > 0 } }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = ROOT.xiangqi_selected_piece_type }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_best_attack_index }
        }
    }
}

# Government is attacking Reformist, so we evaluate Reformist piece vars on the square.
xiangqi_ai_eval_reformist_target_at_tmp_square = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 0 }

    # Must be occupied by enemy at all (enemy for Government = nhanvan/Reformist)
    if = {
        limit = {
            xiangqi_is_square_occupied = yes
            xiangqi_is_square_occupied_by_nhanvan = yes
        }

        # Canon
        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Canon_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 10 }
        }
        # Chariot
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Chariot_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 9 }
        }
        # Horse
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Horse_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 7 }
        }
        # Elephant
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Elephant_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 6 }
        }
        # Soldier Front
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_Front_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 5 }
        }
        # Soldier
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_Soldier_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 4 }
        }
        # General
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_alive = 1 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_row = xiangqi_tmp_check_row }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_Reformist_General_piece_col = xiangqi_tmp_check_col }
            }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_exists = 1 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_hp = ROOT.xiangqi_Reformist_General_health_point }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_target_value = 8 }
        }
    }
}

xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = {
    # Expects:
    #   xiangqi_selected_piece_type already set (1/2/3/4/5/6/7)
    # Assumes Government side
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }

    # Generate legal moves for this type
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_canon_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_chariot_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_horse_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_elephant_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_Soldier_Front_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_soldier_generate_legal_moves = yes } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_government_general_generate_legal_moves = yes } }

    # If none, bail
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count < 1 } }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
    }
    else = {
        # Score moves
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_move_index = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_score = -999 }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        while_loop_effect = {
            break = break
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter < 25 } }

            # stop if past count
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter > xiangqi_legal_move_count } }
                set_temp_variable = { break = 1 }
            }

            # Resolve candidate row/col for this iter
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 0 }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 0 }

            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_1_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 2 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_2_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 3 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_3_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 4 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_4_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 5 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_5_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 6 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_6_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 7 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_7_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 8 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_8_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 9 } }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_row }  set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_9_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 10 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_10_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 11 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_11_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 12 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_12_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 13 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_13_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 14 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_14_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 15 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_15_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 16 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_16_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 17 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_17_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 18 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_18_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 19 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_19_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 20 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_20_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 21 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_21_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 22 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_22_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 23 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_23_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 24 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = ROOT.xiangqi_legal_move_24_col } }

            # ---- Score it ----
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 0 }
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -2 } }

            # 1) Backtrack check (reject/huge penalty if equals last square)
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 0 }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_Canon_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_Canon_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
