                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_Chariot_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_Chariot_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_Horse_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_Horse_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_Elephant_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_Elephant_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_Soldier_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_Soldier_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = xiangqi_Government_General_last_row }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_Government_General_last_col }
                    }
                    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 }
                }
            }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_backtrack = 1 } }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -100 }
            }

            # 2) Prefer forward progress for Government (LARGER col = better)
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = 0 }

            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_curr_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col } }

            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > xiangqi_ai_curr_col } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 3 } }
            else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = xiangqi_ai_curr_col } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }
            else = { add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = -1 } }

            # 3) Mild center preference (row closer to 5 is better)
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 5 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 2 } }
            else_if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 4 } check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 6 } } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }

            # 4) Random tie-breaker (0..1)
            random = { chance = 50 add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score = 1 } }

            # Keep best
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_score > xiangqi_ai_best_score } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_score = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_score }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_move_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_iter }
            }

            add_to_variable = { xiangqi_ai_iter = 1 }
        }

        # If we found anything, execute it
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_best_move_index > 0 } }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_best_move_index }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_move = yes }
            set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
        }

        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
    }
}

xiangqi_ai_government_take_turn = {
    # Reset AI state
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }

    # --------------------------
    # PHASE 1: TRY ATTACKS
    # --------------------------
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_attack_for_type = yes } }

    # --------------------------
    # PHASE 2: IF NO ATTACK, TRY MOVES
    # --------------------------
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } }

        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 6 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 5 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 4 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 3 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 2 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 7 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 0 } check_variable = { xiangqi_Government_General_piece_alive = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 1 } hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_try_move_for_type = yes } }
    }

    # --------------------------
    # PHASE 3: EXECUTE CHOSEN ACTION
    # --------------------------
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 1 }
        }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_execute_chosen_attack = yes }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_found_action = 1 }
            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_action_is_attack = 0 }
        }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_execute_chosen_move = yes }
    }
}

# Mirror of reformist execute chosen move, but side=1 and origin from Government_* vars.
xiangqi_ai_government_execute_chosen_move = {
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 1 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_chosen_type }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = 0 }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row }       set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col } }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_prev_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_row }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_prev_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

    hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_generate_for_selected_type = yes }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_legal_move_count > 0 } }

        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index < 1 } }
            hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_government_choose_nonreversal_move_index = yes }
        }

        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_move_index = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_chosen_index }
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_apply_selected_piece_move = yes }

        if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
        else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_row = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_col = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_prev_col } }
    }
    else = {
        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_clear_legal_moves = yes }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_type = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_selected_piece_side = 0 }
        set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }
    }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 0 }
}

xiangqi_ai_government_choose_nonreversal_move_index = {
    # Same as reformist chooser, but origin is Government vars and forward is delta > 0.

    set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 0 }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = 0 }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_1_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_2_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_3_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_4_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_5_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_6_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col = ROOT.xiangqi_ai_last_square_type_7_col } }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = 0 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = 0 }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_General_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_row } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_Front_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 3 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_row }       set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Elephant_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 4 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Horse_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Chariot_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_row }          set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Canon_piece_col } }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_row = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_row }        set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_origin_col = ROOT.xiangqi_Government_Soldier_piece_col } }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_score = -999 }
    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_index = 0 }

    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    set_variable = { break_loop = 0 }

    while_loop_effect = {
        break = break
        limit = {
            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter < 25 }
            check_variable = { break_loop = 0 }
        }

        hidden_effect = { xiangqi_ai_get_legal_move_at_index = yes }

        if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row > 0 }
                check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col > 0 }
            }

            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 0 }

            # (A) reversal penalty
            if = {
                limit = {
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_row > 0 }
                    check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_last_col > 0 }
                }

                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_row }
                subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_last_row }
                if = {
                    limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = 0 } }

                    set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
                    subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_last_col }
                    if = {
                        limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_cmp = 0 } }
                        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = -100 }
                    }
                }
            }

            # (B) Government advances by INCREASING col => delta > 0 is good
            set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_target_col }
            subtract_from_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_origin_col }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta > 0 } }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 2 }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 7 }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 7 }
                }
                else = { add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 4 } }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_delta < 0 } }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = -3 }
            }

            # (C) centralize row
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 5 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 3 } }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 4 }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 6 }
                    }
                }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 2 }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 3 }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_row = 7 }
                    }
                }
                add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 1 }
            }

            # (D) prefer center lanes for chariot/canon
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 5 }
                        check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 6 }
                    }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 5 }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 6 }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 2 }
                }
                else_if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 4 }
                            check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_target_col = 7 }
                        }
                    }
                    add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = 1 }
                }
            }

            # (E) discourage General wandering
            if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_type = 1 } } add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score = -2 } }

            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_score > xiangqi_tmp_best_score } }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_score = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_score }
                set_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_index = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_iter }
            }
        }

        add_to_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_iter = 1 }
    }

    if = { limit = { check_variable = { xiangqi_tmp_best_index > 0 } } set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = ROOT.xiangqi_tmp_best_index } }
    else = { set_variable = { xiangqi_ai_chosen_index = 1 } }
}
xiangqi_ai_take_turn = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = Playing_Nhan_Van_VIN
		}
		xiangqi_ai_government_take_turn = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = Playing_Government_VIN
		}
		xiangqi_ai_reformist_take_turn = yes
	}
	else = { }
}
add_nhan_van_minigame_to_ui = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VIN
			date > 1955.1.1
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Nhan_Van_Affair}
		}
		add_to_array = {
			country_politics_list = 333	#Nhan Van Minigame
		}
		set_global_flag = Nhan_Van_Affair
	}
}
add_nhan_van_minigame_to_ui_debug = {
	add_to_array = {
		country_politics_list = 333	#Nhan Van Minigame
	}
}
remove_nhan_van_minigame_to_ui = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Nhan_Van_Affair}
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			country_politics_list = 333	#Nhan Van Minigame
		}
	}
}

# Apply once per army-modernisation upgrade.
VIN_improve_drv_army = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = Improve_DRV_Army_TT
	# Equipment, supply and border warfare.
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_ECR value = 0.02 }

	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_OSP value = -0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_HA value = -0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_WA value = -0.01 }

	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_SR value = 0.01 }

	# Lower garrison losses as organisation and control improve.
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_RDG value = -0.01 }

	# Army administration and mobilisation.
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_DCP value = 0.05 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_MS value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_RPF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_RR value = 0.005 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_TT value = -0.01 }

	# Land combat doctrine and field performance.
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_AF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_CAF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_DF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_MF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_OF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_OR value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_CDR value = 0.02 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_DSF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_MD value = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_MPF value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_PP value = -0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_TPR value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VIN_M_A_PS value = 0.01 }
}

# ============================================================
# DRV AIR FORCE
# ============================================================

# Run once when the air-force dynamic modifier is first granted.
VIN_initialize_drv_air_force = {
	add_dynamic_modifier = {
		modifier = VIN_DRV_air_force_research
	}

	# Air combat baseline: a tiny, inexperienced air arm.
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_AAF = 0.75 }		# Air accidents: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_AAG = -0.50 }		# Ace generation: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_AMXP = 0.05 }		# Air mission XP: good
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_ANP = 0.30 }		# Night penalty: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_ATXP = 0.05 }		# Training XP: good
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_AWP = 0.30 }		# Weather penalty: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_AF_AXPL = 0.10 }		# XP lost on wing death: bad
}

# Generic air-force development step.
# Use for focuses such as pilot schools, imported aircraft doctrine,
# Soviet/Chinese advisers, airfield construction, etc.
VIN_improve_drv_air_force = {
	# Fewer crashes as training, maintenance and airfield procedures improve.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_AAF
		value = -0.10
	}

	# More capable pilots and better mission doctrine.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_AAG
		value = 0.10
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_AMXP
		value = 0.03
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_ATXP
		value = 0.03
	}

	# Reduce operational penalties.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_ANP
		value = -0.05
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_AWP
		value = -0.05
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_AF_AXPL
		value = -0.02
	}
}

# Run once when the naval dynamic modifier is first granted.
VIN_initialize_drv_navy = {
	add_dynamic_modifier = {
		modifier = VIN_DRV_navy
	}

	# Navy baseline: coastal irregular capability, limited doctrine and coordination.
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_NDC = 0.50 }		# Naval doctrine cost: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_EGF = 0.00 }		# Navy XP gain
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_SFC = 0.02 }		# Special forces cap

	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_NIC = 5 }			# Naval invasion capacity
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_AI = 0.25 }		# Amphibious invasion modifier
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_IP = -0.10 }		# Invasion preparation: good

	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_NC = -0.15 }		# Naval coordination: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_ND = -0.10 }		# Naval detection: bad
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_SE = 0.05 }		# Screening efficiency
	set_variable = { VIN_M_N_SC = 0.10 }		# Spotting chance
}

# Generic naval-development step.
# Use for focuses involving coastal flotillas, naval schools,
# amphibious doctrine, Chinese/Soviet advisers, or shipyard expansion.
VIN_improve_drv_navy = {
	# Better access to doctrine and more accumulated institutional experience.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_NDC
		value = -0.05
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_EGF
		value = 0.05
	}

	# Expand the pool available for marines / naval special formations.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_SFC
		value = 0.01
	}

	# Improve the ability to land, sustain and coordinate amphibious operations.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_NIC
		value = 2
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_AI
		value = 0.05
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_IP
		value = -0.02
	}

	# Bring the fleet from dispersed coastal units toward a coordinated force.
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_NC
		value = 0.05
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_ND
		value = 0.05
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_SE
		value = 0.02
	}
	add_to_variable = {
		var = VIN_M_N_SC
		value = 0.03
	}
}

# --- AI Helpers ---

# AI-only political power relief, fired from on_monthly_VIE.
# The Etat du Vietnam's PP drains through 1949-53 are intentional, but the AI
# cannot prioritize spending like a player and ends up too broke to take
# coup-avoidance decisions on historical. Top the AI up toward a small floor
# each month instead of a flat income buff so it never banks a surplus and
# large negative PP hits (coup fallout etc.) still take months to recover.
# No event, no decision, no tooltip: invisible to the player, and is_ai is
# re-checked every pulse so a hotjoining player stops receiving it.
VIE_ai_monthly_pp_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = Indochina_War_Over }
			date < 1954.1.1
			check_variable = { political_power < 50 }
		}
		add_political_power = 25
	}
}

# Keeps the faction's name in step with Saigon's own: every VIE cosmetic-tag
# change (and drop) should call this. Names mirror the cosmetic-tag country
# names in VIE_misc_l_english.yml; the bare tag reads "Republic of Vietnam"
# (the Diem-era drop_cosmetic_tag rename). VIE scope, faction leader only.
# The guard flag is set when the 60s "Anti-Communist League" replaces the
# original faction, whose name must then stop tracking the country's.
VIE_sync_faction_name = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VIE
			is_faction_leader = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_generic_faction_created }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_FRA_FED }
			set_faction_name = "Federal Republic of Vietnam"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_FRA_PROV }
			set_faction_name = "Provisional Central Government of Vietnam"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_CuongDe }
			set_faction_name = "People's State of Vietnam"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_NUF }
			set_faction_name = "National United Front for the Liberation of Vietnam"
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_FRA_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_RCB_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_RCB_FRA_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_CCC_ETAT
					has_cosmetic_tag = VIE_CCC_FRA_ETAT
				}
			}
			set_faction_name = "State of Vietnam"
		}
		else = {
			set_faction_name = "Republic of Vietnam"
		}
	}
}

VIE_create_CEFEO_FFL_templates = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VIE_CEFEO_FFL_Templates_Created }
		}
		set_country_flag = VIE_CEFEO_FFL_Templates_Created
		division_template = {
			name = "Legion Etrangere Francaise"
			template_counter = 250
			priority = 2
			is_locked = no

			regiments = {
				ffl = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ffl = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				ffl = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				ffl = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				ffl = { x = 1 y = 1 }
			}
